knos_CheckAsserts.c
division by zero
  Assertion Expression Handling:
    <variable> <op> <expression>
  Description:
    <variable>    - variable name that delivers actual value
    <op>          - eq ne lt le gt ge  (string compare)
                    == != <  <= >  >=  (integer compare)
                    -->                (assign operator)
                    ==>                (eval assign operator)
    <expression>  - comparison:
                    - expression to check actual value against
                      (assert variables are accessed by $0...$9)
                  - assignment:
                    - number of assert variable to store actual value
                      (variables my be any number from 0 to 9)
  Notes:
    There must be spaces between the <variable>, the <op> and the <expression>.
  Examples:
  - dev/uart1/bufferSize == 2048
  - rxIsr --> 0
    ...
    rxIsr < $0
  - avail --> 1
    avail >= $1-64
